Binned update function:
```
def binned_refine(h, steps, eta, step_range, time_limit):
    h=h.copy(); logh=np.log(np.maximum(h,1e-12))
    best_h=h.copy(); best_score=score_full(h)
    step=0.1
    for it in range(steps):
       check deadline
       a = h[map] / S
       p,M = conv(a), max
       if score<best...
       # active y
       # maybe if M == previous and no progress? continue
       y = compute_weights(p, M)
       d = gradient(a,y)
       # bin gradient
       g = bincount(map, weights=d, minlength=m)
       # maybe divide by sizes? average: g_avg = g/sizes
       mass = sizes*h
       z = g/sizes - np.sum(mass*(g/sizes))/np.sum(mass)
       if std(z)>0: z/=std(z)
       # line search
       for lam in [1,0.5,0.25,0.1] if failed:
          logh_new = logh - step*lam*z
          h_new = np.exp(logh_new - max); h_new=np.maximum(h_new,1e-12); h_new/=dot(sizes,h_new)
          score=eval_h(h_new)
          if score<best_score: accept; break
       else step*=0.6
    return best_h
```

def optimize_bins_coord(h, sweeps, eval_budget):

   improved=False

```
def score_h(h):
    S = sizes @ h  # maybe h sum weighted
    if S<=0: return inf
    a = (h * invS)[map_arr]  # multiply full by invS
    p=conv(a)
    M=p.max()

```
def full_refine(a, deadline, max_iter=5000):
    a = a/a.sum(); loga = np.log(a + 1e-15)
    best=a; best_score=score_full(a)
    step=0.2
    no=0
    for it in range(max_iter):
       if time > deadline_break: break
       score = best_score
       p, M = conv_score_full(a)
       # active weights
       if M<=0: break
       det = max(M*1e-4, 1e-12)
       y = np.zeros(L); active = p >= M - det
       # If too many active? If all, use all; okay.
       count = active.sum(); if count==0: y[L//2]=1
       y[active]=1/count
       d = cross_grad(a,y)
       z = d - np.mean(d)
       sd = z.std(); if sd>0: z/=sd
       # gradient proposal
       proposed = None
       for lam in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1]:
          loga_n = loga - step*lam*z
          a_n = np.exp(loga_n - np.max(loga_n))
          a_n = np.maximum(a_n, 1e-12)
          a_n /= a_n.sum()
          sc = score_full(a_n)
          if sc < best_score*(1-1e-12):
              proposed=a_n; prop_sc=sc; break
       if proposed is not None:
          a=proposed; loga=log(a+1e-15); best=a; best_score=prop_sc; no=0; step=min(0.5,step*1.05)
       else:
          # random block perturbation
          cand = random_block(a, loga?, rng, step_rand)
          sc=score
          if sc<best... accept
          else step*=0.8; no+=1
          if no>50: reset step=0.2; no=0
```

Detailed functions:
```
def _npow2(x):
    p=1
    while p<x: p<<=1
    return p

def make_conv_funcs(n):
    L=2*n-1
    nfft=_npow2(3*n-2)
    def conv(a):
        F=np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
        c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=nfft)
        return c[:L]
    def score(a):
        a=np.asarray(a,float)
        s=a.sum()
        if s<=1e-300 or not finite: return inf
        p=conv(a)
        m=p.max()
        if m<0: m=0
        return 2*n*m/(s*s)
    return ...
```
Scale invariant, but if a not normalized maybe big; a values from b sum1, h normalized.

For score_h, we can avoid convolution allocation? Use conv. To speed, precompute `map_arr` and in score_h:
```
def score_h(h):
    S = sizes @ h
    if S<=0: return inf
    a = h[map_arr]  # length n, maybe view? np indexing returns copy
    # optional scale by 1/S for score? not needed if use S. But to avoid overflow, if S large/small: scale =1/S; a = h[map_arr]*scale
    p=conv(a)
    M=p.max()
    return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```
If h normalized S≈1, a small. But in coordinate tests, after h_try/=S, S=1, so can simplify: maintain h normalized to S=1. Then score_h = 2n*max(conv(h[map])) since sum=1. However due normalization, a.sum() exactly S? a.sum() = sizes @ h =1. Good. We'll enforce.

def score_h(h):
    a = h[map_arr]
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=nfft)

    if not np.isfinite(maxp): return inf
    return 2.0 * n * maxp

Coordinate optimization memory: `h_try = base.copy()` inside factor loop 9*128*many, small. Python loops many; use local variables for speed.

```
def coord_opt(h, deadline, n, map_arr, sizes, eval_h, rng):
    m=len(h)
    def norm_h(h): S=float(sizes @ h); if S<=0 or not finite: return None; return h/S
    h0=norm_h(h); if None: h0=np.ones(m)/np.dot(sizes,ones)
    best=h0; best_s=eval_h(best); cur=best.copy(); cur_s=best_s
    coarse = np.array([0.5,0.7,0.85,0.95,1.05,1.15,1.4,1.8,2.5])
    fine = np.array([0.9,0.95,1.05,1.1])
    no_sweeps=0
    sweep=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       sweep +=1
       factors = coarse if sweep % 3 !=0 else fine  # or switch after 10
       imp=False
       order=np.random.permutation(m) # slower? use rng.integers
       for j in order:
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           base=cur
           old=base[j]
           best_local_s=cur_s
           best_local=base
           for r in factors:
               ht=base.copy()
               ht[j]=old*r
               S = float(sizes @ ht)
               if S <=0: continue
               # normalize? eval_h assumes normalized? We can make eval take unnormalized with dot. Simpler eval_h_norm expects normalized. Divide:
               ht /= S
               sc = eval_h(ht)
               if sc < best_local_s - 1e-15:
                   best_local_s=sc; best_local=ht
           if best_local is not base:
               cur=best_local; cur_s=best_local_s; imp=True
               if cur_s < best_s:
                   best=cur.copy(); best_s=cur_s
           # else no change
       if imp:
           no_sweeps=0
       else:
           no_sweeps +=1
           if no_sweeps >=5 or cur_s > best_s*1.1:
               # random restart around best
               cur=best.copy()
               noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.25,size=m))
               cur *= noise
               S=float(sizes@cur)
               if S>0: cur/=S; cur_s=eval_h(cur)
               else: cur=best; cur_s=best_s
               no_sweeps=0
               if cur_s > best_s*1.5: cur=best; cur_s=best_s
       if best_s < 1.49 and sweep>20:
           # good enough? could break to refine
           break
    return best,best_s
```
But note `best_local is not base` will be False even if best_local is a modified copy but score same. We need flag best_r !=1. We can set `best_ht = None`. Simpler:

Full refine details:
```
def full_refine(a, deadline, rng):
   a=a/a.sum(); best=a.copy(); best_s=eval_full(a)
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15))
   step=0.2
   no=0
   t_vec = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   for it in range(100000):
      if time> deadline: break
      # gradient step
      p = conv(a); M=p.max()
      if M<=0: break
      tol=max(1e-8, M*1e-4)
      y=np.zeros(L); active = p >= M - tol
      if active.sum()==0: y[L//2]=1
      else: y[active]=1/active.sum()
      d=grad(a,y)
      z=d - d.mean()
      sd=z.std()
      if sd>0: z/=sd
      if step>1e-4:
         accepted=False
         for lam in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
             la=loga - step*lam*z
             a2=np.exp(la - la.max())
             a2=np.maximum(a2,1e-12); s=a2.sum(); if s<=0: continue
             a2/=s
             sc=eval_full(a2)
             if sc < best_s*(1-1e-10):
                 a=a2; loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-15)); best=a.copy(); best_s=sc; accepted=True; step=min(0.5,step*1.03); no=0; break
         if not accepted:
             # random move
             for _ in range(2):
               cand=random_cand(a, rng, t_vec)
               sc=eval_full(cand)
               if sc < best_s*(1-1e-10): accept; break
             if not accepted:
                 step*=0.8; no+=1
                 if no>30:
                    step=0.2; no=0
                    # random restart around best
                    if rng.random()<0.5:
                       # gradient direction random restart?
                       pass
      else: break
   return best,best_s
```
This may be slow due random moves each call. We can limit full refine if best internal already <1.5? Still okay.

Random full candidate function:
```
def random_full(a, rng, t_vec, n):
    cand=a.copy()
    mode = int(rng.integers(3))
    if mode==0:
       w=int(rng.integers(5, max(10,n//4)))
       st=int(rng.integers(0,n-w+1))
       cand[st:st+w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.1)))
    elif mode==1:
       r=int(rng.integers(1,5)); amp=0.1; ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       cand *= 1 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*r*t_vec + ph)
    else:
       # multiply a few indices
       k=int(rng.integers(5, min(50,n)))
       idx=rng.choice(n,k,size=False,replace=False)
       cand[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.15,size=k))
    np.clip(cand,1e-12, None, out=cand)
    s=cand.sum()
    if s<=0: cand=a.copy(); s=1
    cand/=s
    return cand
```
`rng.choice` each time may be slow; use `idx=rng.integers(0,n,k,endpoint=False)`.

def _make_b(n):
    a=np.empty(n, dtype=float)
    val=1.0
    a[0]=val
    for i in range(1,n):
        val *= (2*i-1)/(2*i)
        a[i]=val
    s=a.sum()
    return a/s if s>0 else np.ones(n)/n
```

def random_bins(rng, style=0):
   w = rng.normal(0,0.5,m)
   # smooth twice
   w[1:-1]=(w[0:-2]+w[1:-1]+w[2:]) /3
   w[1:-1]=(w[0:-2]+w[1:-1]+w[2:]) /3
   if style==0: power=-0.5*np.log(np.arange(m)+1)
   elif style==1: power=-0.3*np.arange(m)/m
   else: power=np.zeros(m)
   h=np.exp(power + 0.5*w)

```python
import numpy as np
import time

_TARGET = 1.50274349


def _next_pow2(x: int) -> int:
    x = int(max(1, x))
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _make_binomial_profile(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """c_i = binom(2i, i) / 4^i, normalized to sum 1."""
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = 1.0
    a[0] = v
    for i in range(1, n):
        v *= (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
        a[i] = v
    s = a.sum()
    if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
        a /= s
    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 6.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Use the supplied construction length when sensible; otherwise choose a
    # medium length with O(n log n) FFT cost fitting the resource budget.
    try:
        n_in = int(np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).size)
    except Exception:
        n_in = 2535
    if not (16 <= n_in <= 20000):
        n_in = 2535
    n = n_in
    L = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)

    if budget <= 12.0:
        return [float(x) for x in _make_binomial_profile(n)]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Fast internal scoring (FFT convolution). The final candidate is
    # verified with the authoritative evaluate_sequence() before return.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    rfft = np.fft.rfft
    irfft = np.fft.irfft

    def _full_conv(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        A = rfft(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), n=nfft)
        c = irfft(A * A, n=nfft)
        return c[:L]

    def _score_full(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all():
            return float("inf")
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300:
            return float("inf")
        a = a / s
        p = _full_conv(a)
        m = float(np.max(p))
        if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
            m = 0.0 if m == -0.0 else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(m):
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * n * m)

    def _grad_full(a: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Subgradient (up to a positive factor) of max_lagaranyl coefficient.
        For active weights y_k, derivative wrt a_i is
          2 * sum_{r=0}^{n-1} a_r y_{i+r}.
        The constant 2 is irrelevant for relative updates.
        """
        if n == 1:
            return np.array([float(a[0] * max(0.0, float(y[0])))], dtype=np.float64)
        ya = rfft(a, n=nfft)
        yrev = y[::-1]
        yb = rfft(yrev, n=nfft)
        c = irfft(ya * yb, n=nfft)
        idx = np.arange(2 * n - 2, n - 3, -1)
        return c[idx]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Non-uniform bins: fine near the left endpoint, coarser to the right.
    # This matches the expected flat-convolution profile (mass concentrated
    # near one edge), while remaining low-dimensional for search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    m = int(min(128, max(16, n // 16)))
    raw_sizes = np.linspace(0.25, 5.0, m)
    sizes = np.maximum(1, np.round(raw_sizes * (float(n) / raw_sizes.sum())).astype(np.int64))
    # Adjust to exact length.
    diff = n - int(sizes.sum())
    while diff < 0:
        j = int(np.argmax(sizes))
        if sizes[j] <= 1:
            break
        sizes[j] -= 1
        diff += 1
    while diff > 0:
        j = int(np.argmax(sizes))
        sizes[j] += 1
        diff -= 1
    map_arr = np.repeat(np.arange(m, dtype=np.int64), sizes)

    def _score_h(h: np.ndarray) -> float:
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
        if h.size != m or not np.isfinite(h).all():
            return float("inf")
        S = float(np.dot(sizes, h))
        if S <= 1e-300:
            return float("inf")
        a = h[map_arr] * (1.0 / S)
        p = _full_conv(a)
        mv = float(np.max(p))
        if not np.isfinite(mv) or mv < 0.0:
            mv = 0.0 if mv == -0.0 else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(mv):
            return float("inf")
        return float(2.0 * n * mv)

    def _norm_h(h: np.ndarray):
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        h[h <= 0.0] = 1e-16
        S = float(np.dot(sizes, h))
        if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
            return None
        return h / S

    def _expand_h(h: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        S = float(np.dot(sizes, h))
        if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
            return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        a = h[map_arr] * (1.0 / S)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s2):
            a /= s2
        else:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        return a

    def _full_to_bins(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        h = np.bincount(map_arr, weights=a, minlength=m).astype(np.float64)
        h /= sizes.astype(np.float64)
        h = np.maximum(h, 1e-14)
        q = _norm_h(h)
        return q if q is not None else _norm_h(np.ones(m))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial candidates.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    best_full = _make_binomial_profile(n)
    best_s = _score_full(best_full)

    # A small deterministic grid over log-polynomial profiles.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
        lt = np.log(np.maximum(t, 1e-12))
        l1 = np.log(np.maximum(1.0 - t, 1e-12))
        alphas = (-0.62, -0.58, -0.55, -0.52, -0.50, -0.47, -0.44,
                  -0.40, -0.35, -0.30, -0.20, -0.10, 0.0, 0.1, 0.2)
        betas = (-0.30, -0.20, -0.10, -0.04, 0.0, 0.05, 0.12, 0.20, 0.30, 0.45)
        for al in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            for be in betas:
                try:
                    h = np.exp(al * lt + be * l1)
                    if not np.isfinite(h).all():
                        continue
                    s = float(h.sum())
                    if s <= 0.0:
                        continue
                    a = h / s
                    sc = _score_full(a)
                    if sc < best_s:
                        best_s = sc
                        best_full = a
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Initial bin profiles: use the best full profile, a pure log-power,
    # and two smooth random profiles.
    start_bins = []
    try:
        hb = _full_to_bins(best_full)
        scan = _score_h(hb)
        if np.isfinite(scan):
            start_bins.append((scan, hb))
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        hp = np.exp(-0.5 * np.log(np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0))
        hp = _norm_h(hp)
        scan = _score_h(hp)
        if np.isfinite(scan):
            start_bins.append((scan, hp))
    except Exception:
        pass

    for style in (0, 1):
        try:
            w = rng.normal(0.0, 0.55, size=m)
            w[1:-1] = (w[:-2] + w[1:-1] + w[2:]) / 3.0
            w[1:-1] = (w[:-2] + w[1:-1] + w[2:]) / 3.0
            if style == 0:
                base = -0.5 * np.log(np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0)
            else:
                base = -0.35 * (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, m - 1))
            hr = np.exp(base + 0.65 * w)
            hr = _norm_h(hr)
            scan = _score_h(hr)
            if np.isfinite(scan):
                start_bins.append((scan, hr))
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not start_bins:
        start_bins.append((2.0, _norm_h(np.ones(m))))

    start_bins.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
    h0 = start_bins[0][1].copy()
    h_best = h0.copy()
    h_best_s = _score_h(h_best)
    if not np.isfinite(h_best_s):
        h_best_s = 2.0

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Binned coordinate descent (multiplicative scaling of one bin at a time).
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _optimize_bins(h0: np.ndarray, stop_time: float):
        cur = _norm_h(h0)
        if cur is None:
            cur = _norm_h(np.ones(m))
        cur_s = _score_h(cur)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_s):
            cur_s = 1e9

        best = cur.copy()
        best_s = cur_s

        coarse = (0.45, 0.60, 0.75, 0.88, 0.94, 1.07, 1.16, 1.32, 1.65, 2.20, 3.20)
        fine = (0.82, 0.90, 0.955, 1.05, 1.12, 1.24)

        no_improve = 0
        sweep = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stop_time:
            sweep += 1
            factors = coarse if (sweep % 4 != 0) else fine
            sweep_improved = False

            order = rng.permutation(m)
            for j in order:
                if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                    break

                base = cur
                old = float(base[j])
                best_local = base
                best_local_s = cur_s

                for r in factors:
                    ht = base.copy()
                    ht[j] = old * float(r)
                    S = float(np.dot(sizes, ht))
                    if S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
                        continue
                    ht /= S
                    try:
                        sc = _score_h(ht)
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_local_s * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                        best_local_s = sc
                        best_local = ht

                if best_local is not base:
                    cur = best_local
                    cur_s = best_local_s
                    sweep_improved = True
                    if cur_s < best_s:
                        best = cur.copy()
                        best_s = cur_s

            if sweep_improved:
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                no_improve += 1
                # Periodic randomized escape around the best binned profile.
                if no_improve >= 5 or cur_s > best_s * 1.15:
                    try:
                        cand_best = best.copy()
                        noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.35, size=m))
                        cand = cand_best * noise
                        cand = _norm_h(cand)
                        if cand is not None:
                            cand_s = _score_h(cand)
                            # Allow modest uphill moves to leave local basins.
                            if np.isfinite(cand_s) and (
                                cand_s < best_s * 1.04 or rng.random() < 0.07
                            ):
                                cur = cand
                                cur_s = cand_s
                                if cur_s < best_s:
                                    best = cur.copy()
                                    best_s = cur_s
                            else:
                                cur = best.copy()
                                cur_s = best_s
                        else:
                            cur = best.copy()
                            cur_s = best_s
                    except Exception:
                        cur = best.copy()
                        cur_s = best_s
                    no_improve = 0

            # Enough headroom for the remaining stages?
            if best_s < _TARGET - 0.01 and sweep > 30:
                break

        return best, best_s

    # Reserve time for full-vector refinement and final exact scoring.
    full_reserve = min(220.0, max(25.0, 0.18 * budget))
    final_reserve = min(30.0, max(8.0, 0.05 * budget))
    bin_stop = deadline - full_reserve - final_reserve
    if bin_stop <= t0:
        bin_stop = deadline - max(2.0, 0.02 * budget)

    try:
        h_best, h_best_s = _optimize_bins(h0, bin_stop)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # If a different start had a good score, give it a chance if time remains.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - full_reserve - final_reserve - 8.0:
        for _, h_alt in start_bins[1:3]:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - full_reserve - final_reserve - 8.0:
                break
            try:
                stop = min(
                    deadline - full_reserve - final_reserve - 8.0,
                    time.monotonic() + min(120.0, max(10.0, 0.25 * budget)),
                )
                h_try, s_try = _optimize_bins(h_alt, stop)
                if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < h_best_s:
                    h_best = h_try
                    h_best_s = s_try
            except Exception:
                pass

    best_full = _expand_h(h_best)
    best_s = _score_full(best_full)
    if not np.isfinite(best_s):
        best_full = _make_binomial_profile(n)
        best_s = _score_full(best_full)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Full-vector entropy-based local refinement.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    full_stop = deadline - final_reserve

    def _random_full(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        cand = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
        if mode == 0:
            w = int(rng.integers(5, max(10, n // 8)))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
            cand[st:st + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.09)))
        elif mode == 1:
            k = int(rng.integers(4, min(80, n)))
            idx = rng.integers(0, n, size=k, endpoint=False)
            cand[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.14, size=k))
        elif mode == 2:
            r = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            cand *= 1.0 + 0.10 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * r * tt + ph)
        else:
            # Split a block and mildly swap mass.
            w = int(rng.integers(8, max(16, n // 10)))
            st = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - 2 * w + 1)))
            a1 = cand[st:st + w].copy()
            a2 = cand[st + w:st + 2 * w].copy()
            mix = 0.25
            cand[st:st + w] = (1.0 - mix) * a1 + mix * a2
            cand[st + w:st + 2 * w] = mix * a1 + (1.0 - mix) * a2

        cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-14)
        s = float(cand.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            cand = np.array(a, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
            s = float(cand.sum())
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            cand /= s
        return cand

    def _refine_full(a0: np.ndarray, stop_time: float):
        a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        else:
            a = a / s
        best = a.copy()
        best_s = _score_full(a)
        if not np.isfinite(best_s):
            return a, best_s

        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-16))
        step = 0.22
        no_progress = 0
        it = 0

        while time.monotonic() < stop_time and step > 1e-5:
            it += 1
            try:
                p = _full_conv(a)
                M = float(np.max(p))
                if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
                    break
                tol = max(1e-12, 1e-4 * M)
                active = p >= (M - tol)
                cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))
                if cnt == 0:
                    active = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                    active[L // 2] = 1.0
                    cnt = 1
                y = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
                y[active] = 1.0 / cnt

                d = _grad_full(a, y)
                if not np.isfinite(d).all():
                    break
                z = d - float(np.mean(d))
                sd = float(np.std(z))
                if sd <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sd):
                    break
                z = z / sd

                accepted = False
                for lam in (1.0, 0.55, 0.30, 0.14):
                    if time.monotonic() >= stop_time:
                        break
                    la = loga - step * lam * z
                    if not np.isfinite(la).all():
                        continue
                    an = np.exp(la - float(np.max(la)))
                    an = np.maximum(an, 1e-14)
                    s2 = float(an.sum())
                    if s2 <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s2):
                        continue
                    an /= s2
                    sc = _score_full(an)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                        a = an
                        best = an.copy()
                        best_s = sc
                        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-16))
                        step = min(0.45, step * 1.04)
                        no_progress = 0
                        accepted = True
                        break

                if not accepted:
                    # One exploratory random move.
                    cand = _random_full(a)
                    sc = _score_full(cand)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_s * (1.0 - 1e-10):
                        a = cand
                        best = a.copy()
                        best_s = sc
                        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-16))
                        no_progress = 0
                    else:
                        step *= 0.80
                        no_progress += 1
                        if no_progress >= 35:
                            step = 0.22
                            no_progress = 0
                            # Occasionally recenter around the best full vector
                            # with a small multiplicative perturbation.
                            if rng.random() < 0.35:
                                tmp = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=n))
                                tmp = np.maximum(tmp, 1e-14)
                                s3 = float(tmp.sum())
                                if s3 > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s3):
                                    tmp /= s3
                                    s3score = _score_full(tmp)
                                    if np.isfinite(s3score) and s3score < best_s * 1.03:
                                        a = tmp
                                        loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-16))
            except Exception:
                # Preserve best and stop gracefully if the refinement destabilizes.
                break

        return best, best_s

    if time.monotonic() < full_stop:
        try:
            refined, rs = _refine_full(best_full, full_stop)
            if np.isfinite(rs) and rs < best_s:
                best_full = refined
                best_s = rs
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final exact verification with the authoritative evaluator.
    # Ensure we always return a valid finite list before the hard deadline.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    final_stop = deadline - 1.5
    candidates = []

    def _add_candidate(arr):
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
            if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
                return
            arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if not np.isfinite(arr).all():
                return
            if float(arr.sum()) < 0.01:
                return
            lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
            candidates.append((lst, arr.mean().bit_length() if hasattr(arr, 'mean') else 0))
        except Exception:
            return

    _add_candidate(best_full)
    _add_candidate(_make_binomial_profile(n))
    _add_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size == n and np.isfinite(parent).all():
            _add_candidate(parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    best_list = None
    best_exact = float("inf")

    # Deduplicate short versions very coarsely to avoid spending exact time
    # on identical arrays.
    seen = set()
    for lst, _ in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= final_stop:
            break
        # Low-cost fingerprint: length, sum, max, a few samples.
        try:
            a = np.asarray(lst, dtype=np.float64)
            key = (
                len(a),
                round(float(a.sum()), 12),
                round(float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0, 12),
                a[0] if a.size else 0.0,
                a[-1] if a.size else 0.0,
                a[len(a) // 2] if a.size else 0.0,
            )
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_exact:
                best_exact = sc
                best_list = lst
        except Exception:
            continue

    if best_list is None:
        # Absolute fallback: guaranteed finite, nonnegative, valid.
        best_list = [1.0 / n for _ in range(n)]

    return best_list
```
